# Documentation coverage and evidence ledger

[Architecture index](README.md) · [Modules](modules.md) · [API contracts](api-contracts.md)

## Scope and run contract

- Repository: public `hsoliwal/rewrite`, fork/source relationship `openrewrite/rewrite`
- Source revision: `0c82f71bc8bb215a8194db59a3784551d6a60e99`; source Git tree: `066b2180752282207da5e2a9a98d192e2b7a74df`
- Existing draft destination: [fork PR #5](https://github.com/hsoliwal/rewrite/pull/5), branch `docs/fork-contributor-guide-20261014`, base `main`
- Allowed delta: editorial Markdown documentation and navigation in this fork
- Locked: source/API/external behavior, disabled and excluded code paths, generated outputs, build/dependency/workflow files, licenses and upstream provenance
- Method: complete returned recursive tree, manifest inventory, one representative source declaration per source-directory group, main-source package-info review, selected complete API bodies and existing documentation
- Stop condition for this pass: documentation extension recorded and diff/readback checked; unresolved semantic and execution work remains explicit. This is not a completed M3 transformation/proof run
- Publication review: public repository evidence only; no private uploaded doctrine, conversations or unrelated repository details are included

## Accounting is not substantive coverage

| Level / measured set | Discovery/accounting | Documentation state |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Repository | 1 included public fork, exact source baseline recorded | 1 partial; 0 reviewed-complete |
| Explicit Gradle projects/modules | 34 distinct names from settings; all have module sections | 34 partial responsibility/build/dependency descriptions; 0 reviewed-complete |
| Other build units | Root, buildSrc, 3 C# projects, 1 Go module, 1 npm runtime, 1 Python distribution, 2 Maven sidecars; structural Gradle parent and .NET solution separately identified | 10 additional described build units; structural containers described; 0 reviewed-complete |
| Auxiliary npm configuration | 1 .rewrite/package.json with no scripts/entry point | Included inventory; standalone-project intent unresolved |
| Fixture manifests | 73 nested npm/Python manifests | Included as test inputs with fixture provenance; not silently counted as independent shipping projects |
| Source-directory groups | 572 groups containing 5,026 files with the listed source extensions; every group has a row below | 330 main/benchmark/native groups have partial responsibility summaries; 242 test/fixture/other groups have inventory/source references only |
| Observed primary declarations | 259 Java, 6 Kotlin, 2 Scala, 14 C#, 21 Go group samples; 9 Python package areas, 18 TypeScript areas and 1 unnamed Groovy area | These are group/sample counts, not a complete logical namespace census; duplicates across source sets/artifacts are retained |
| Main-source package-info.java | 191 files read; only 2 have package Javadoc prose | 189 declaration/annotation-only files do not count as semantic documentation; no inline edits made |
| Native file modules | 153 TypeScript + 98 Python files under production src roots | Package-area summaries only; per-file module/API contracts remain incomplete |
| Important API leaves | Selected parser/recipe/visitor/table/RPC/DSL/sidecar bodies inspected | Bounded contract notes; no complete exported-member denominator or semantic-proof claim |

No aggregate coverage percentage is reported: namespace identity across every file, fixture-project semantics and every API/file-module contract are not fully reconciled. **Reviewed-complete is zero**, even where useful draft prose exists, because complete semantic reconciliation and applicable verification are outstanding. Ordinary production areas remain included; missing detail is not relabeled an exclusion. There are no Git submodule entries in the returned tree.

### Generated, test, fixture and auxiliary treatment

- Generated ANTLR parser directories remain in the ledger. Some contain handwritten base helpers as well; the group's generated label does not imply every file can be overwritten. Grammar inputs and generateAntlrSources tasks are their provenance.
- Java TCK main packages and java-test fixtures are test support, not ordinary production recipe APIs. Native test helpers can be shipped as library support while the runnable suites remain separate.
- [PDM](../../rewrite-python/src/test/resources/pdmlock/README.md), [Poetry](../../rewrite-python/src/test/resources/poetrylock/README.md) and [uv](../../rewrite-python/src/test/resources/uvlock/README.md) fixture catalogs explain generated lock/manifest pairs, scenario boundaries and recorded responses. Historical generation dates/results in those catalogs were not rerun here. Preserve their byte-sensitive contents.
- npm lock fixtures under rewrite-javascript/rewrite/test/javascript/fixtures are test data. The additional .rewrite/package.json is auxiliary dependency configuration with unresolved standalone intent.
- .moderne/context is existing generated analysis, not the current coverage authority. Its architecture overview was consulted before source inspection but only lists a small external-service graph, insufficient for this module/package inventory. Missing database/service-endpoint context files were not invented.
- .github, .idea, .run, .claude, gradle, doc and root configuration are support/metadata areas. Scripts and generators were read only where relevant, never executed.

## Concrete open gaps

1. **Inline package semantics**: 189 of 191 main-source package-info files have no package-level Javadoc prose. Type-level docs may still be useful, but nullness defaults do not explain a package's responsibilities/contracts.
2. **Go parser drift**: GoModParser's class Javadoc describes PlainText/includeSumHashes, while parseInputs delegates to Go RPC and requests GoMod. See [the contract note](api-contracts.md#gomodparser-current-implementation-versus-stale-comment). Java comments were not modified under the Markdown-only scope.
3. **Declaration-only namespaces**: rewrite-java/filter, rewrite-json/marker and rewrite-kotlin/search have only package-info; the C# remote directory's lone file declares org.openrewrite.remote.csharp rather than its directory-shaped org.openrewrite.csharp.remote. These are stubs/declarations, not documented implementations.
4. **Native instructions drift**: C# guidance has an older test directory and bridge-task description; current build/tree uses the sibling OpenRewrite.Tests and integTest. Python tox lists py311 while its package requires >=3.12. The Java TCK README also names a Java 17 IDE runtime while its current manifest adds Java 21. This guide distinguishes those historical instructions from current source; source/configuration changes remain outside this pass.
5. **Package identity completeness**: representative declarations establish observed namespaces, but a directory can contain another declaration/namespace. The 572 groups are not substituted for the unresolved complete package count.
6. **Native file modules**: the 251 Python/TypeScript files have path identities below; individual contracts, exports, dependency edges and error/resource/concurrency semantics are not all documented.
7. **API leaves and tests**: only selected bodies were inspected in depth; public-member census, inline reconciliation and exact test-to-contract mappings remain open. Related test-source links are navigation only.
8. **Verification**: Gradle/Maven/native installs, lint, compilation, tests, integration/corpus runs, benchmarks, generators and rendered docs were not executed. No historical assistant text, source test or benchmark is promoted to a fresh pass.

| ID | Stage | Result | Evidence / limitation |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| V-TREE | GitHub recursive tree read at source revision | PASS for path retrieval | 7,144 entries; truncated=false; 5,916 blobs; no submodule entries |
| V-MANIFEST | Read identified manifests and settings | PASS for source retrieval | 120 manifest files including analysis-pom.xml; native/configuration/fixture classification below |
| V-SAMPLE | Representative declaration/package-info inspection | PASS for bounded source retrieval | 572 source-group representatives; 191 main-source package-info files; not exhaustive member review |
| V-LINK | Editorial link/path reconciliation | PASS for static targets | Inspected 2,943 Markdown link destinations; all repository-relative and pinned-source paths/new-page anchors resolved against the pinned tree and proposed files after correcting two anchors. External URLs and rendering were not verified |
| V-DIFF | Final remote diff/readback and PR state | Recorded in PR | The PR description records the resulting documentation head and connector verification; no build result is implied |
| V-LINT | Documentation linter/rendering | NOT_RUN | No repository tool execution in this connector-only editorial pass |
| V-COMPILE | Gradle, Maven, dotnet, Go, TypeScript, Python compilation/package checks | NOT_RUN | Source-derived instructions only; no dependency/toolchain resolution performed |
| V-TEST | Unit/TCK/integration/native/corpus tests | NOT_RUN | Linked test files are evidence of test definitions only |
| V-RUNTIME | Generators, benchmarks and runtime use | NOT_RUN | No measurements or runtime-qualification claims |

## Source-directory evidence ledger

IDs S001–S572 are path-group identities, not an assertion that a directory equals a package. Namespace is the declaration in the inspected representative (or a source-area identity for Python/TypeScript). A source group can mix production/test files in Go or generated/handwritten grammar code. All rows are included. Primary rows link a partial package guide; other rows are inventory-only with semantic work still missing.
